# Skills · A · part 149

851 entries, aipt-method-delegated-authority to airl-collect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **aipt-method-delegated-authority**: Delegation, approval, accept a proposal, authority check, who may approve, request human approval, expiry, revocation, version binding, self-authorization. Apply a recorded human delegation to one versioned proposal and return accepted, rejected, deferred or human_required, binding any acceptance to the exact proposal version, hash, scope and expiry. Acceptance is not execution permission; a role cannot grant itself authority; a same-user local approval file is a claim, not proof. Not for ruling on evidence (aipt-method-evidentiary-adjudication) or writing the decision record afterward. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-delegated-authority) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-discovery-interrogation**: Discovery questions, problem reconstruction, decision-critical gaps, consequential questions before design. Use when a request is underspecified, touches workflows, trust, permissions, architecture or operating model, and solving the wrong problem is expensive. Reconstructs the problem from supplied sources, sorts gaps by what they would change, and returns only questions whose answers would change direction, scope, risk handling or success measures. Not for complete specs, trivial gaps, or when the requester has declined questions. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-discovery-interrogation) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-drift-and-regression-audit**: Drift audit, regression check, parity, baseline versus changed, invariants, allowed deltas, hidden drift, migration, refactor, rewrite, consolidation. Compare a baseline version and a changed version of an artifact or behavior against declared invariants and allowed deltas; classify each difference as exact parity, intentional change, ambiguous, regression or hidden drift; root-cause regressions and name the minimum repair or the decision that would bless the deviation. Use after a migration, refactor or rewrite when parity matters. Not for greenfield work with no baseline. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-drift-and-regression-audit) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-evaluation-data-curation**: Evaluation dataset, split manifest, development vs held-out, sampling frame, slice coverage, permissioned sources, synthetic data labeling, public fixture allowlist. Use to build the versioned case set a frozen evaluation plan needs: representative cases from permitted sources, a deterministic development/held-out split, per-slice counts against the plan's minimums, stated sampling limits, and no confidential record in any public fixture. Synthetic cases are always labeled synthetic. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-evaluation-data-curation) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-evaluation-execution-and-receipts**: Run evaluations, execution receipts, exit status, frozen versions, fixture_replay vs live, timeout vs failure, skipped vs passed, command logs. Use to execute checks or evaluations against frozen plan, dataset and candidate versions and to record for each command the arguments, working directory, snapshot, data and configuration versions, exit status, timeout state, skipped counts and log locations. A receipt proves the command's exit status only. Replay is never labeled live. A command mentioned is not a command run. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-evaluation-execution-and-receipts) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-evaluation-planning**: Evaluation plan, prespecified metrics, failure slices, pass thresholds, baseline, freeze before results. Use before any candidate result exists to write and freeze what an evaluation measures, on which slices, against which threshold and baseline, and how the analysis runs. A later change is a disclosed amendment proposal reported beside the original criteria, never a retroactive pass. Average accuracy does not establish safety on a protected slice. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-evaluation-planning) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-evidence-and-lineage**: Evidence records, source scope, claim typing, lineage links, contested claims, impact policy, immutable versions. Use when recording a source, stating or assessing a claim, linking evidence to a requirement, decision, artifact or validation, handling a contradiction, or working out what a changed version affects. Produces source, claim and link records whose scope and limitations every summary inherits; a contradiction becomes a contested assessment and a review task, never a status flip. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-evidence-and-lineage) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-evidentiary-adjudication**: Adjudicate, rule on evidence, judge sufficiency, resolve a disagreement, converge, compare options against criteria, admissible evidence, insufficient evidence, unresolved. Rule on whether admissible evidence carries a stated claim and how options compare against frozen criteria, with a finding per objection and one outcome: option_acceptable, option_rejected, insufficient_evidence, unresolved or next_test_required. Never decide by vote count or confidence rhetoric. Not for writing challenges (aipt-method-adversarial-challenge) or applying delegation (aipt-method-delegated-authority). ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-evidentiary-adjudication) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-independent-audit**: Independent audit, verification, frozen artifact, acceptance criteria, re-run checks, pass fail blocked, reviewer independence. Use when a delivered artifact with a pinned version must be verified against its actual acceptance criteria by someone who did not produce it, in a fresh context. Re-runs or replays the checks under receipts, inspects the files directly, and returns a review record with pass, fail or blocked and the evidence for each criterion. Not for drift comparison, structural audits, evaluation campaigns or ruling on disputed evidence. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-independent-audit) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-milestone-planning**: Milestone planning, work orders, execution order, write sets, dependencies, acceptance checks. Use when approved requirements and an authorizing decision exist and the work must be split into bounded controller work orders that a builder can implement and verify in one loop. Produces a dependency-ordered plan in which each order declares scope, write set, prerequisites, acceptance checks and the receipts it must produce. Not for writing requirements, implementing a change or staging a release. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-milestone-planning) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-outcome-and-value-analysis**: Outcome hypothesis, value analysis, option comparison, doing nothing, non-AI alternative, baseline, association versus effect, denominators, confounders, reporting changes, complaints versus harms. Use before building to state the outcome that matters as a falsifiable hypothesis and compare options, including doing nothing and non-AI options, on one fixed set of criteria; use after release to judge whether observed outcomes show an effect rather than an association. Checks denominators and reporting changes, and never reads fewer complaints as fewer harms. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-outcome-and-value-analysis) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-requirements-and-acceptance**: Requirements, acceptance criteria, protected requirement, requirement-to-check map, negative case, correct refusal, unknown outcome. Use when turning accepted scope and decisions into atomic versioned requirements, deciding whether a requirement is protected, writing the observable checks that would show a requirement met or violated, or when someone offers "a response exists" as proof. Produces requirement records, an open-decisions list and an acceptance map; a failed implementation never retires a protected requirement. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-requirements-and-acceptance) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-research-consolidation**: Research consolidation, staged research, source inventory, extraction, synthesis, verification, provenance, conflicting evidence, coverage statement. Run research over a multi-source corpus in five gated phases (inventory, extraction, consolidation, synthesis, verification) so that every claim keeps its sources, conflicts stay visible and uncertainty is stated rather than smoothed over. Use when the result will become need findings, a comparison, a shortlist, a specification, a plan or a governance artifact. Not for quick lookups, light summaries or brainstorming. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-research-consolidation) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-retrieval-and-context-contracts**: Retrieval contract, context assembly, authorization before exposure, scope inheritance, workspace isolation, freshness, negative access test, prompt-only hiding. Use when specifying or reviewing how an AI feature acquires information: which sources it may read, how permission is checked before any content reaches the model, how scope survives summaries, embeddings, indexes and caches, how stale content is handled, how retrieved items are ordered and sized in context, and which denial tests prove each scope boundary. Produces a retrieval contract artifact and a negative test list. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-retrieval-and-context-contracts) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-rollout-and-operations**: Staged rollout, release plan, cohort, canary, stopping conditions, rollback, kill switch, alert ownership, runbook, operational readiness. Use when a validated artifact version needs a plan for reaching users in stages, saying who is in each cohort, what is observed, what stops the stage, how the change is reverted and who owns each alert. Produces the plan and readiness conditions only; deployment and publication stay outside autonomous scope, and rollback counts as available only after it has been exercised in a synthetic environment with a receipt. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-rollout-and-operations) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-scoped-implementation**: Scoped implementation, builder loop, smallest safe diff, work order, targeted checks, receipts, files changed. Use when one authorized work order with a write set, acceptance criteria and a pinned snapshot must be turned into a verified change. Inspects before editing, keeps the diff inside the write set, runs the targeted checks under receipts and reports exact files, checks, receipts and residual risks. Not for planning the work, auditing it, or running an evaluation campaign. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-scoped-implementation) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-security-and-privacy-review**: Security review, privacy review, permission enforcement, trust boundary, injection exposure, scope inheritance, egress, retention and removal, enforced control versus requested compliance, export ruling. Use when reviewing an AI feature's design or code for where permissions are enforced, where untrusted content enters model input, where scoped data travels and leaves, and whether removal works, or ruling on whether a private record may be published. Produces a review with an assurance level per control, findings with verification requirements; escalates legal questions and never certifies. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-security-and-privacy-review) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-status-handoff**: Status handoff, status brief, current state, resume, blockers, next queue, latest validated progress. Use when a human or a fresh session must resume a case quickly and needs a short, current brief derived from controller state: run state, active work orders, latest validated progress with applicability, blockers with next actions and the immediate queue. The controller renders the brief; this method decides what goes in and in what order. Not for the evidence ledger, research synthesis, outcome analysis or the full case report. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-status-handoff) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-structural-integrity-audit**: Structural audit, integrity check, duplication, contradiction, bloat, filler, portability, readiness verdict. Use before a set is installed or published, or when generated variants need cleanup. Not for single-file edits or baseline-versus-change comparison. ([3orionkings/ai-product-team](https://github.com/3orionkings/ai-product-team/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/.claude/skills/aipt-method-structural-integrity-audit) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **aipt-method-tool-and-permission-boundaries**: Tool contract, permission boundary, idempotency key, timeout, retry policy, proposed action versus executed action, enforceable boundary versus requested compliance. Use when defining or reviewing the tools an AI feature may call: typed inputs, results and errors; idempotency keys for non-idempotent effects; timeouts and retry rules; separating actions the model proposes from actions the system executes; binding each effect to target identifiers and versions; and labeling each boundary as enforced by code or merely requested of the model. Produces tool contract and action record artifacts.
